One previously documented medium conditional oracle issue remains reproducible, and acquisition custody contracts remain unsuitable for funding because their execution/outflow paths are incomplete. Deployment-specific dependencies and launch configuration remain unverified.
   6: 
   7: This is a source-review conclusion, not deployment or activation clearance. Production source was patched locally and regression tests were added. No transaction was broadcast, and no push or merge is claimed.
   8: 
   9: ## Exact scope
  10: 
  11: Repository HEAD: `e56cf576065c5f1bbb9bb55115f5961267f7d654`. The working tree is dirty and includes untracked genesis source; HEAD alone does not identify the reviewed implementation. [Source hashes](source-hashes.json) bind all 97 Solidity files under `src`, including interfaces and vendored auction code. The original source-drift check found zero before remediation. Those retained hashes identify the pre-remediation baseline; refreshed final source/artifact pins and verification are pending and must not be inferred from historical hashes.
  12: 
  13: The [manifest](REVIEW-MANIFEST.json), [compiler settings](compiler-settings.json), [dependency hashes](dependency-hashes.json), [artifact hashes](artifact-hashes.json), and [test inventory](test-inventory.json) identify the build and scope. Canonical compilation uses Solidity 0.8.26, Cancun, optimizer 800, and the repository's per-file IR restrictions. The captured source artifacts have maximum runtime size 23,220 bytes and maximum creation-bytecode size 26,573 bytes; constructor arguments and actual deployment configuration still need independent verification.
  14: 
  15: Included: tokens/staking/fees, NFTs/vouchers/gear/deeds, stock/RWA custody and registries, bank/collateral/liquidity funding, acquisition modules, gas pools, genesis lifecycle/oracles, every current market module, player sale/coordinator/guarded auction, and the vendored dependency closure. Source review followed imported authorization, callback, transfer and accounting paths. Vendored/library review and detector coverage are not independent formal proofs of every dependency function.
  16: 
  17: Excluded from release claims: actual chain addresses, live runtime/proxy/Safe state, production assets/adapters/ERC4626 vaults, broad off-chain signer/indexer/database implementation review, and operational launch rehearsal. The named local stock-delivery integration and unsigned deployment-plan checks below were exercised. No specific production configuration was supplied for this review.
  18: 
  19: ## Methods and independent reviews
  20: 
  21: The repository's [security review policy](../../SECURITY-REVIEW-POLICY.md) supplied the pinned Pashov, Plamen and Trail of Bits method revisions. Applied adversarial access-control, execution, accounting/invariant, economic and boundary passes; trust-boundary/context mapping; property-test quality checks; concrete harm/proof requirements; and per-diagnostic static triage. Available slots and Windows/native tools replaced upstream orchestration details explicitly; no claim is made that twelve upstream agents, Medusa, Echidna or formal verification ran.
  22: 
  23: Daybreak ran through the installed Codex CLI using `gpt-daybreak-blue-latest`; its complete independent [report](daybreak-report.md) and [execution transcript](evidence/daybreak-run.txt) are retained. That initial CLI invocation used its configured low reasoning effort. The completed second high-effort [cross-check](daybreak-crosscheck.md) is retained with [its transcript](evidence/daybreak-crosscheck-run.txt). It identified StreetDeed burn/remint identity mutation, generic CCA fee-quote underflow and extreme oracle period overflow, plus evidence/triage corrections. Its separate full-suite attempt collided with shared Foundry artifacts and is not counted as a pass. The primary run supplies fresh complete-suite evidence separately. Historic evidence cited by Daybreak remains historical.
  24: 
  25: Additional retained source reviews: [core](core-review.md), [current market/genesis](market-review.md), [system model](context.md), and [entry points](context-entrypoints.md). Checks covered reentrancy/callbacks, authorization/replay, rounding/conservation, oracle history/freshness/liquidity, configuration/lifecycle recovery, and privilege/economic boundaries.
  26: 
  27: ## Findings and dispositions
  28: 
  29: ### RT-M02 / historical M-02 - medium, conditional unsupported oracle price
  30: 
  31: Affected path: `OmertaHook`  `OmrV4TwapOracle`, when an initialized pool has zero active liquidity. Initialization does not require liquidity; a zero-delta swap can change its tick; the hook accrues that tick; the oracle later publishes the resulting interval as fresh.
  32: 
  33: The executed `ComprehensiveMarketAudit.test_risk_emptyLiquidityProducesZeroCostFreshTwap` moves square-root price from Q96 to 2xQ96 without paying either asset and observes approximately 4x the original OMR/ETH quote. This establishes unsupported-price publication. It does **not** prove theft from a deployed bond: an economic-loss path additionally needs a consumer trusting that feed, absent liquidity and issuance/authorization conditions that permit use of the quote.
  34: 
  35: Disposition: **open, conditional; no clearance for consumers of this oracle under absent liquidity**. This historical finding was preserved rather than rewritten as a newly discovered exploit. The current market's observation/state path rejects zero-liquidity epochs; this does not clear other consumers. Remediation for any activation of the affected feed must validate liquidity across the entire observation interval, or use an independently depth-qualified feed and retest the consumer. A spot-only liquidity check would not establish that interval property.
  36: 
  37: ### RT-P01 - acquisition funding/activation blocker
  38: 
  39: `AcquisitionVault`/`AcquisitionVaultCore` accept custody/accounting ingress but lack a complete corresponding asset release path. The authority's unpause fails closed and intent execution/reconciliation contain staged surfaces. Slither's two locked-ether diagnostics are accurate implementation/phase limitations, not false positives.
  40: 
  41: Disposition: **do not fund or activate these rails as a functioning acquisition system**. Existing local guards do not supply a withdrawal facility. No privileged or public theft was demonstrated. Completing execution/outflow changes reopens review.
  42: 
  43: ### Configuration and operational residuals
  44: 
  45: - Immutable genesis-splitter recipients must accept ETH. One permanently rejecting recipient blocks the atomic distribution. No attacker-controlled redirection was established. ETH held before pool initialization is permissionlessly recoverable to the fixed treasury; pre-migration funding order must match this intended behavior.
  46: - Player-sale settlement is permissionless and per-wallet; all participants must settle before proceeds release. Eligibility cardinality, keeper enumeration and deadline capacity need launch-specific rehearsal. Cancellation preserves the tested refund path; unavoidable/profitable settlement DoS was not demonstrated.
  47: - Codehash pinning verifies runtime identity, not proxy implementation/storage immutability or semantic correctness. Actual manager, Permit2, auction, hook, Safe and asset selections require verification.
  48: - Standard-token and selected ERC4626 valuation/withdrawal behavior are external assumptions. Fee-on-transfer, rebasing, callbacks and upgrades need configuration-specific validation.
  49: - Turf adjudication, signer decisions, health feeds and governance remain explicit trusted authorities. No privilege-escalation path was confirmed; correctness of off-chain truth was outside scope.
  50: - Cancun transient-storage support is a deployment prerequisite for the vendored guard.
  51: 
  52: These conditions are not silently accepted on the owner's behalf and prevent a universal deployment-ready conclusion.
  53: 
  54: ## Closed hypotheses
  55: 
  56: The [market review](market-review.md) retains the actual dependency trace showing position-manager mutation requires current NFT-owner/approval authority, while transfer into the commitment vault clears token approval. Depositor/operator permissions cannot remove locked position liquidity through that manager. Invalid/stale observation anchors cannot backfill rewards; maturity withdrawal does not depend on oracle state.
  57: 
  58: The extreme genesis inversion input is excluded by the documented auction floor and monotone clearing price. Matching-reserve adequacy remains an explicit configuration requirement with integer/finite-range margin; no inadequate supported-configuration witness was established. The report retains the reserve calculation rather than claiming unspecified final parameters are safe.
  59: 
  60: Mythril's three high arithmetic diagnostics target optimized comparisons in `cap(uint8)`. The new [exhaustive test](../../test/audit/PlayerSaleCapDomain.t.sol) checks every uint8 input against the five published caps or the exact eligibility error and passes. Five environment/deadline diagnostics are intentional phase predicates. [Symbolic triage](mythril-triage.json) preserves each warning. Runtime-only symbolic execution starts with unconstrained storage and was bounded to 60 seconds, two transactions and depth 32; it is not a whole-contract proof.
  61: 
  62: ## Executed verification
  63: 
  64: Pre-remediation complete Foundry results: **1,275 tests across 88 suites passed, zero failures/skips, exit 0**, with 512 fuzz cases per fuzz test and invariant budget 128 histories x 128 calls, seed `0x20261001`. [Complete-suite output](evidence/forge-full-bounded.txt). These baseline totals and exit status remain recorded in the manifest; they do not validate the subsequent patches. The initial [default-budget output](evidence/forge-full.txt) uses 512 invariant histories x 500 calls and reached 1,273 passing tests before deliberate interruption while its final stock-registry invariant remained active. It is **partial evidence, not a complete-suite pass**; no failure was observed before interruption. The same invariant passed in both completed fixed-seed campaigns. Passing tests are evidence for their assertions and generated histories, not every possible execution.
  65: 
  66: A separate same-seed reproducible campaign used seed `0x20261001`, 64 invariant runs and depth 64: **26 invariants across 16 suites passed, zero failures/skips**. [Output](evidence/fixed-seed-invariants.txt). The exhaustive cap regression passed separately. [Output](evidence/player-cap-domain.txt). The repository sets `fail_on_revert=false`; handlers intentionally reject/catch invalid operations. These conservation/authorization properties do not establish that every possible operation is live, or that mocked external feeds match a production deployment.
  67: 
  68: Slither 0.11.6: **53 successful JSON scans, 837 diagnostic occurrences, 689 unique IDs, initially labeled** in [static triage](static-triage.md) and [per-ID records](static-triage.json). Initial missing-PATH and split-build-info failures are retained. Daybreak correctly identified two unrelated disposition rationales (Stability range and guarded batch claim); labels alone are insufficient, and corrected substantive triage/final refresh must be retained before completion. Five native stack-depth failures were superseded by explicit IR scans. Native source-detector scans differ from canonical per-file IR settings and are not bytecode-equivalence evidence. Dependency presence findings are retained without implying unused helpers are independently proven.
  69:

[Output](evidence/forge-sizes.txt). Its 448 Forge lint warnings were separately retained and dispositioned: 182 production, 19 vendor, five scripts and 242 test warnings. All unchecked transfer/call warnings occur in tests. [Lint triage](forge-lint-triage.md), [per-warning records](forge-lint-triage.json). Local Anvil stock integration passed 15 asserted steps, including signed deed mint, canonical token-bound account derivation and actual stock delivery. [Output](evidence/stock-e2e.txt). The unsigned liquidity deployment-plan suite passed 19 checks using deterministic RPC mocks. [Output](evidence/liquidity-deployment-plan.txt).
  73:

After local dependency/service preparation, DEX integration completed **23 asserted steps** using real router/PositionManager encodings ([final output](evidence/dexbot-e2e-final.txt)); PostgreSQL integration completed **19 E2E assertions and 11 actual keeper transactions** against isolated local services ([output](evidence/liquidity-e2e.txt)). Intermediate bootstrap/warmup clock and stale bond-expectation failures remain retained under their distinct filenames. These resolved local integration results complement stock delivery's 15 steps and unsigned deployment plan's 19 checks; they do not validate production infrastructure or replace final combined-source regressions.
  75: 
  76: ## Change control
  77: 
  78: The conclusion applies only to the retained file hashes, compiler settings and source-review phase. Four local remediation classes are recorded below; final combined-source validation is pending. The conditional medium finding and dormant custody limitations remain open. Material source, dependency, signer, oracle, authorization or launch-configuration changes require reassessment and affected checks. Daybreak correctly identified the initially missing evidence-manifest.json; the parent must generate and verify final SHA-256 evidence binding before completion. No existing manifest binding is claimed here.
  79: 
  80: ## Local remediation and pending final verification
  81: 
  82: 1. **StreetDeed immutable district:** preserve original name/district through burn and remint; a fresh signed voucher changing district now reverts. This is signer-dependent integrity, not unprivileged theft. Retain the pre-fix failing proof (`evidence/deed-before-fix.txt` and its exit record), original source/test snapshots, and focused regression output. Do not overwrite those with post-fix logs.
  83: 2. **Oracle maximum periods:** both oracle constructors reject periods above `type(uint32).max / MAX_WINDOW_MULT` (1,073,741,823 seconds), preventing accepted parameters from overflowing the update window multiplier. This is an extreme trusted-configuration liveness boundary; ordinary configured periods retain their semantics.
  84: 3. **Vendored CCA fee quote:** initialization parameter reads clamp protocol fees to the raise, matching existing sweep behavior. An excessive nonzero fee controller previously caused arithmetic panic; current guarded auction fixes its controller to zero. This remedy restores quote/sweep consistency and cannot supply liquidity capital when net proceeds are zero. Original vendor pins/upstream hashes remain provenance; manifest/README explicitly record the local patch. Focused generic-auction regressions cover excessive fee and zero-controller behavior.
  85: 4. **Full wall-time Hook/oracle baselines:** Hook accumulation records full last-write time while preserving wrapped external timestamp/cumulative ABI; both oracle baselines track full wall time to discard an interval spanning a uint32 timestamp cycle instead of treating its wrapped residue as fresh. Long-window rebasing precedes short-window predicates. This is distinct from the retained unsupported empty-liquidity price condition; the latter remains open.
  86: 
  87: Parent coordinates the final full suite and source/artifact/evidence refresh after all patches. Current remediation tests and final size/static results must remain **pending** until their recorded exit statuses are available. Earlier 1,275 passing tests, 26 same-seed invariants and prior size/static results are pre-remediation evidence, never a substituted final pass. RT-M02 and RT-P01 remain phase blockers for their affected legacy feed/consumers and staged acquisition custody. No universal release-ready, pushed or merged conclusion is made.
